## E338 — 공동신탁의 첫 창

CONTEXT STATUS: **FULL — SOURCE-BOUND DEEP EXECUTION PACK**
CONTEXT READINESS: **READY**
Source Card: [[ga4-e338-345-episode-cards-v1]]
Date: CY 743-10-12.
POV / information source: Rema/Ardis window operators, vessel masters, service recipients and evidence custodians.
HIGH_WATCH_BAND: `N/A`

### Common six-field contract

**ACTIVE_DESIRE_MAIN**  
execute the first independently authorized Joint Trust window and launch the evidence mission without exceeding recovery or political scope.

**ACTIVE_DESIRE_SECONDARY**  
success can be used to claim the Trust no longer needs Imperial/Continuity support; failure can justify external takeover.

**PHYSICAL_ANCHOR / WORK-SYSTEM CARRIER**
- [APPROVED SECTION CARRIER EVIDENCE] [Scene units] 1. **Physical start** — B-2 holds 14%; B-4 direct/direct/physical sources agree; no claimant or protagonist root key is used. 2. **Inbound phase** — 324 t and 58 people enter under food/medical/service priority; no dense module, weapons or claimant court cargo. 3. **Outbound phase** — three mission vessels, 86 people and 28 t evidence/repair equipment leave under separate masters and custody chains. 4. **Hard close** — total 352 t and 144 people clear; operators close rather than use the remaining 8 t nominal capacity. Decisive choice: preserve the approved mission and node margin instead of adding politically valuable cargo. Reward: the Joint Trust proves it can authorize a bounded routine window without Rian, Iris, Haren or one claimant’s signature. Cost: B-2 falls to 11–12%; no repeat window for an estimated 11–17 days; route timing is exposed to all claimant blocs … [source continues]

**STATE_CHANGE**
- the Joint Trust proves it can authorize a bounded routine window without Rian, Iris, Haren or one claimant’s signature.

**COST_OR_REFUSAL**

Decisive choice: use dynastic/Registry legitimacy as a bounded evidentiary service rather than a claim to immediate total rule … [source continues]

Date: CY 743-11-11.

HIGH_WATCH_BAND: `N/A`

### Common six-field contract

**ACTIVE_DESIRE_MAIN**  
establish standing, custody and question order before any party converts evidence presentation into accession.

**ACTIVE_DESIRE_SECONDARY**  
limiting standing can make the hearing manageable while excluding the people and services that will live under the remedy.

**PHYSICAL_ANCHOR / WORK-SYSTEM CARRIER**
- [APPROVED SECTION CARRIER EVIDENCE] [Scene units] 1. **Four claimant standing fields** — Elian: Registry/heir/ceremonial continuity; Mara: civil/regency administration; Dor: military/protector security; Isa: Continuity/root-key/service custody. 2. **Additional standing** — provincial consultation bodies, founding-covenant/protected constituencies, Ardis as evidence/current-service actor, Neutral medical/claims, Vesper as hull/lien claimant and Baseline survivors as people—not evidence objects. 3. **Witness protection** — Tera may testify only on physical actions she observed; medical advocate can stop; no repeated claimant interrogation; memory gaps and rehabilitation needs remain. 4. **Adjudicator exclusions** — Rian cannot judge or supply a future/Archive answer; he may testify later only on current mission/evidence handling. Reward: high-dependency patients and oxygen/water service remain safe; 2.6 t is salvaged lawfully … [source continues]

- [APPROVED SECTION CARRIER EVIDENCE] [Scene units] 1. **Proposed default** — after 12 minutes without field concurrence, sovereign mandate becomes provisional order unless a direct safety stop exists. 2. **Failure replay** — Meridian shows current physical information can be late, absent or compressed; silence is not consent and direct stops do not cover every service/rights conflict. 3. **Central case** — GL-4/M-4 show faster common decisions can reduce spoilage and restore services. 4. **Revision** — timeout produces narrowest preauthorized safe/service action or hold, explicit cost and emergency review; sovereign may publish recommendation and accountability statement but gains no default actuation. Decisive choice: reject silence-as-sovereign-consent even at the cost of slower unresolved emergencies. Reward: the vacant office cannot become a hidden tie-break through timeouts … [source continues]

**STATE_CHANGE**
- the vacant office cannot become a hidden tie-break through timeouts.

**COST_OR_REFUSAL**
- the compact still lacks one actor who can force a choice when safe/service minima conflict.

**REENTRY_ANCHOR**
- centralists shift to the last capture path: let the sovereign define when an emergency exists and which fields are relevant.

Decisive choice: distribute nomination across body types and public evidence rather than one dynasty, claimant office or simple majority. Reward: ballot remains open but bounded by real standing and separation duties … [source continues]

- ballot remains open but bounded by real standing and separation duties.

**COST_OR_REFUSAL**
- coalition-building and procedural challenges become part of selection.

**REENTRY_ANCHOR**
- negotiators now dispute whether eleven votes can bind only themselves or claim a wider state office.

**CURRENT_ACTOR_GOAL_EVIDENCE**
- Goal evidence only; exact acting owner must remain source-bound: establish nomination standing without making hereditary registration or negotiator majority sufficient alone.

**DECISION_EVIDENCE**

## E434 — 열한 표가 스물넷을 묶지 않는다

CONTEXT STATUS: **FULL — SOURCE-BOUND DEEP EXECUTION PACK**

### Common six-field contract

**ACTIVE_DESIRE_MAIN**

**ACTIVE_DESIRE_SECONDARY**

## E435 — 왕을 세우는 규칙보다 왕을 멈추는 규칙

CONTEXT STATUS: **FULL — SOURCE-BOUND DEEP EXECUTION PACK**

### Common six-field contract

**ACTIVE_DESIRE_MAIN**  
define immediate suspension, recall, vacancy and alternate without creating a second hidden sovereign.

**ACTIVE_DESIRE_SECONDARY**  
easy recall makes the office symbolic and unstable; hard recall permits prestige/representation capture.

**PHYSICAL_ANCHOR / WORK-SYSTEM CARRIER**
- [APPROVED SECTION CARRIER EVIDENCE] [Scene units] 1. **Immediate suspension** — illegal direct actuation, root/key seizure, field-budget starvation, armed/civil command claim or evidence tampering triggers temporary suspension through public evidence + affected/relevant field findings. 2. **Recall** — 8 of current full compact ratifiers after evidence hearing; candidate/occupant retains defense and appeal but no automatic stay of direct safety protections. 3. **Vacancy** — reference functions revert to separate Registry/external/compact attestations; no facilitator or Regent automatically becomes sovereign. 4. **Alternate** — ceremonial/external alternate only, preconfirmed and unable to inherit claimant fields or extend term. - [APPROVED SECTION CARRIER EVIDENCE] [Scene units] 1. **Registered-Heir ruling** — Elian validly inherits Crown Reference, Registry and ceremonial/external succession standing subject to compact scope/ratification; not civil, military, root/service, local physical, affected-user or evidence authority. 2. **Separate-office ruling** — Mara civil, Dor military and Isa Continuity functions arise from their own authentic triggers and do not become property of the Crown Reference occupant. 3. **Noninheritable fields** — direct physical, affected-service and public evidence/appeal authority remain current institutional/people functions, not sovereign inheritance. 4. **Stronger sovereign path** — a single executive sovereign may still be newly created by explicit constitutional/compact ratification, but cannot claim automatic inheritance from Registered-Heir succession … [source continues]

**REENTRY_ANCHOR**
